# Native Map

Function-level map of the native Free Realms client (`FreeRealms.exe`). Updated 2026-10-12 00:23.

Every function is either mechanically labelled by an exact rule, or mapped by a DeepSeek worker whose every cited anchor was machine-verified against the Ghidra export, packet catalogue, vault or wiki, then sampled by an independent reviewer worker. Disputed labels are capped (`⚠`). Names are evidence-backed proposals, not original symbols; the original server code does not exist, so server behaviour appears only as labelled hypotheses in [[#Systems]].

## Coverage

| category | functions | share |
|---|---|---|
| rule: EH unwind funclets | 77,904 | 39.1% |
| rule: stubs (return constant) | 5,543 | 2.8% |
| rule: thunks | 1,807 | 0.9% |
| ✅ confirmed | 10,337 | 5.2% |
| 🟢 likely | 12,472 | 6.3% |
| 🟡 speculative | 4,797 | 2.4% |
| ⚪ unknown | 4,629 | 2.3% |
| pending | 81,916 | 41.1% |
| **total** | **199,405** | |

Disputed by review and capped: 4,883. Shards: {'accepted': 262, 'queued': 509, 'review_pending': 64, 'pending': 60, 'external': 6}.
